#### R0.8.4：早闭合时序回归（发现并回退候选参数）

g1 使用代码 commit `98ee643750e5a8cfc3e7b3578657e94e297962fe`、场景 SHA256 `aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a`、控制 SHA256 `80fe0c77449e18d460553600d7e6628a0cef62d80fd9521531602de1282c8bcc`，seed `2026104300–2026104308`，三速度 × y=`−55/0/+55 mm` 各 1 集。strict scorer 未改；所有 9 集均失败，`failure_primary=unstable_grasp`，`first_contact_s` 有值但 lift probe 未启动、hold 为 0。闭合相对固定自由滑动到达参考提前 `122–141 ms`，首次接触发生在 close 后 `15.5–62.0 ms`；接触后测得开口 `0.79–11.68 mm`，均低于 gate 的 `12 mm` 最小单指行程。该结果定位为闭合过早、目标被夹爪挡在可抓区外，而非 lift monitor 误拒。

逐集结果及一个代表失败 trace/GIF 在 [`r0_8_shared_gate_oracle_3speed3lateral_20261017a/`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r0_8_shared_gate_oracle_3speed3lateral_20261017a/)，逐项 `SHA256SUMS` 通过：CSV `8295c5d23e184fd408e05fdebc45cb705a30c5232a41550cf8e53633020d92bf`，summary `06d0fa7fc7f48e7a490de5a1c2f974084b0c8f65fe7f86c6f94e3974d95743b4`，目录清单 `e5a53c9297896a8818b65a55110265db0762269febc3e52719ef903726ec218a`。manifest code/config hashes 同上；runtime Genesis 1.2.2 + Torch 2.8 CPU，0.5 ms physics。wall/user/system/max RSS `241.33/234.48/6.00 s / 1,567,484 KB`，exit 0；运行 log/runtime SHA256 `b771a86354b22af72d9063532a38816dc501a6cf3ade374e97836c8211fc0c04 / f415ce86e8a957176fe572d9a9d2c1bc18ff4a826805b25909be159c3b0e3801`。

此前独立 strict oracle 已证明 `0.08 - 0.10·(v−0.30)` s（截断 `[0.05,0.10]`）在三档有效，预期 close lead 为约 `80/70/60 ms`；R0.8 中改到 `150/140/130 ms` 后九集全失败。故当前配置候选回退至先前经验证的速度规则；新控制文件 SHA256 `3d289c2bef1c562b26f9b713c2a826b813a286a1f7e21f9ead0a01c63c5e0d80`，机械值未动，只改早闭合 lead 参数。须先用新 seed 三档中心各一集确认与 shared gate 兼容，再跑三档 × 左/中/右开发回归；不得将旧 300/300 直接算作新 gate 统计。

#### R0.8.5：回退闭合时序的三速度中心先导

代码 commit `4ac34c63908de5af81e737562b3a5a4c38514161`，control SHA256 `3d289c2bef1c562b26f9b713c2a826b813a286a1f7e21f9ead0a01c63c5e0d80`，scene SHA256 `aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a`，每档新种子一集，初始 y=0，g1 exit 0。0.30 和 0.50 m/s 为 strict success，0.40 m/s 在 full-lift monitor 中止；这是 `2/3` 开发先导，不是统计结论。三集实际 close lead 与自由滑动预测到达时差为 `76.4/67.0/57.4 ms`；三集接触后夹爪净开口约 `39.78 mm`，证实回退已避免 9 集早闭合中的 0.79–11.68 mm 夹死状态。

失败 seed `2026104321`（0.40 m/s）probe 于 `0.705 s` 开始；CSV 记录 lift start `1.125 s`、monitor abort `1.4105 s`。abort 时关节基线漂移 `45.69 µm`，strict relative slip `2.878 mm`，已连续 qualified hold `0.154 s`。由于它在 strict 10 mm slip 上限前停止，现有轨迹无法判断这属于真实早期失稳，还是 0.4 m/s 动态载荷造成的 q 漂移误报。下一步只做一次统一阈值敏感性诊断，并用既有前缘不稳定样本验证负例仍能及时 abort；阈值候选不按 seed 特化。strict scorer 保持不变。

结果目录 [`r0_8_closure_rollback_center_pilot_20261017a/`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r0_8_closure_rollback_center_pilot_20261017a/) 和 `SHA256SUMS` 全项通过。CSV SHA256 `6c3dda222aa3fc6e8d4454404828e3903c868a7cbf766475b02d575aaefb17b8`；summary `37c78688e8f1f9c7dead3051cf774bd25b9b423b54e7d28c803fa0bcb859adf6`；manifest `e7a0d96c874f666a0f9283835b97c5c6744c869cedacd7b13e47211b18cc1073`；目录清单 `d89d6535aad169dac47a69f4f8c4f503c63cdee06e0364162f67dff5402d552c`。runtime Genesis 1.2.2/Torch 2.8 CPU；wall/user/system/max RSS `141.99/132.95/5.25 s / 1,489,600 KB`。运行 log/runtime/launcher SHA256 `34752565ae7fb781b68cb7a91ede9143df72d9ef4d8acc5d2cb081742a9dc932 / 6ea7ae0ad94a7729877013e09c99a79223eefea67e0daee8189241b0514a37e3 / 4c3d34ef8f2e062ed5d236a7cb0d86377518f17080d9d76394449c924cd0a80e`，exit 0。

#### R0.8.6：60 µm monitor 敏感性诊断（非候选）

运行 commit `3481a3251c02f63cbc41b2132a8a743af85100e0`、scene SHA256 `aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a`、诊断控制 SHA256 `59ef2b10b4fd74f06aa35fbd0303448d28b32e0b63798d27bcc7d55bf2f76c42`，只把共同 full-lift q-drift 触发阈值从 `30` 改到 `60 µm`；任务、闭合 lead、速度、姿态和 strict scorer 不变。静态/侧偏 regression exit 0、状态 PASS：center 与 y+15/+20/+25/+30 mm 完成 strict 0.5 s hold；x+25 mm 没有进入 lift probe，x+30 mm 空间 miss；empty close 不 eligible；完整任务 gate 假阳性列表为空。y+20/+25/+30 和 x+25 的 close-only eligibility 仍是进入 probe 的请求，不能单独当作成功或负例。完整样本很小，只证明这组负例下未见 false positive。

同 seed `.4 m/s` dynamic counterfactual `2026104321` 下，60 µm monitor 仍在 full lift 中以 `unstable_grasp` 中止：时间 `1.4385 s`，关节漂移 `63.01 µm`，strict qualified hold `0.2135 s`，严格失败；对照 30 µm 同 seed 运行在 `1.4105 s` 中止，漂移 `45.69 µm`，hold `0.154 s`。阈值调高只延后约 `28 ms`，仍没有完成 strict hold，不能据此认定动态误拒已消除。相较之下 30 µm 对先前不稳定前缘轨迹留出更早中止余量；故主候选保留 30 µm，不采用此 60 µm 诊断配置。

结果 [`r0_8_proprio_monitor60_negative_regression_20261017a/`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r0_8_proprio_monitor60_negative_regression_20261017a/) 的清单已逐项验证：summary `a32d6351718de68bb9b119784e7f059569f078bad19d5a5d1af97194ba21c7c8`，manifest `1b5ff83bdb3a874096ba8f08d08d410eadf351375bffe09972cee182aa2bf05e`，SHA256SUMS `6a24bb50d33a8b836be05dd0a376f27ef64ebc5343aa2a4c727ba4bc41be3ebb`。Python wall/user/system/max RSS `175.00/163.91/6.72 s / 1,512,432 KB`，exit 0；log/runtime/launcher SHA256 `fe2134decfe719c329c077912788799d008c7af8195947f8184d02a95fa7ec9e / cf28f74b2616083c32e723a58b9d5a183dad1b80d0679c61cf1c4391e6bf45f0 / 143c5d8f443ae8ef3a5801ccc76e7ca62fb8885d574239c5f3508ec7a93feaa7`。对应 [`r0_8_monitor60_dynamic_counterfactual_20261017a/`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r0_8_monitor60_dynamic_counterfactual_20261017a/) 清单通过：CSV `320c2eb194d3151b1cd1d7792065cbc6d44191b92ffe2280213fc617a4bc2aac`，summary `fbe175c1070c3b5e055fe0c338024f325b5ebf6c0dd270347547ea4720e826ec`，manifest `a18d6956c8bd1acf3e3aad8a5a840411767e34ce4545b50103c632a973dbd954`，SHA256SUMS `f83c5328c53b64765674c70cfe7864374f21c01da6fd40e3b14b5c7153d2d5db`；Python wall/user/system/max RSS `114.12/90.68/4.54 s / 1,433,664 KB`，exit 0；log/runtime/launcher SHA256 `4fdb603c7b0c2e0dd2ad66c49639da0a3f626f97215b3d9c462f15208836408c / 2510181942134c2bb332ce57a384af75b2a0f5f1b8b1321b62a03025c7b1902d / cdf5fd5b7c6e5a65cfb89ec85ca4835ff9d0b1eea020d56500560a2b1df6059b`。两组 exit file SHA256 均 `9a271f2a916b0b6ee6cecb2426f0b3206ef074578be55d9bc94f6f3fe3ab86aa`。

#### R0.8.7：正式候选 gate + rollback lead 的九集新 oracle

代码 commit `e13b0b307c464e3f753cd3bacaeb092ee5e22586`，scene SHA256 `aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a`，shared-control SHA256 `3d289c2bef1c562b26f9b713c2a826b813a286a1f7e21f9ead0a01c63c5e0d80`，seed `2026104340–4348`，覆盖每档 y=`−55/0/+55 mm` 一集。strict scorer 不变。总成功 `6/9`：0.30 m/s `3/3`、0.40 `1/3`、0.50 `2/3`；Wilson 95% 区间分别 `[0.439,1]`、`[0.061,0.792]`、`[0.208,0.939]`，仅开发诊断，远不足以证明可靠率门槛。所有九集都有目标/夹爪接触且闭合后开口 `39.776–39.820 mm`，没有再次出现 early-close 的 0.79–11.68 mm 小开口问题。闭合相对规划自由滑动到达参考提前约 `51.8–77.9 ms`；首次接触在 close 后 `9.5–15.5 ms`。失败全部由共同 lift monitor 报 `grasp_gate_abort`，无 spatial miss：0.40 m/s y=0 与 +55 mm 分别在 strict qualified hold `0.473/0.1425 s` 后以 q 漂移 `45.33/46.00 µm` 中止；0.50 m/s y=0 在 lift 前的 probe 中漂移 `45.81 µm` 中止。0.40 m/s y=0 的中止仅差 `27 ms` 未达到 strict 连续 0.5 s，构成当前最重要的动态 gate 不确定性。`60 µm` 同 seed counterfactual 仍会 abort，但尚未对上述三个 fail seed 做匹配 counterfactual；不能把 6/9 解释为确定的机械不可达，也不应冻结 30 µm 阈值。

结果 [`r0_8_shared_gate_oracle_3speed3lateral_20261017b/`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r0_8_shared_gate_oracle_3speed3lateral_20261017b/) 清单逐项通过：CSV `62cb2ebbfa8dd3edf3dcea6ea02f80160e6084bfb781fd4490ff165689cf79ec`，summary `ef675d2f74e329298835f4aaa15c0314876d446f654c451fa65f8cdc7e46e6e1`，manifest `d94a4b35b307916b839e5d9269d56ee3e32af2ea5d4d7012efe7fc4325c71324`，SHA256SUMS `0ecfa6e7a25218af9de592dec6477bbb8139722f54e20db4abb344d9e7407a65`。Python wall/user/system/max RSS `205.68/194.55/6.15 s / 1,579,100 KB`，exit 0；log/runtime/launcher SHA256 `aa2d518e87929fda5447287e31508e8264059610f3076e4f8e76ddfaf374a285 / a63ccf9b37311244113395ccd185f3b2fe92781a161e8f58185a59a7b132c130 / 4027a3f592a14ede627e12b51eb6de6d4b7dc5d4528e551cbb6bfed46b0a218a`。

#### R0.8.8：既有 raw-event 的五-bin 1/2 kHz 决策回放（新运行）

g1 使用代码 commit `e13b0b307c464e3f753cd3bacaeb092ee5e22586` 重跑启动器 `r0_8_five_bin_sampling_20261017b.launch.sh`，exit 0；scene SHA256 `aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a`、shared-control SHA256 `3d289c2bef1c562b26f9b713c2a826b813a286a1f7e21f9ead0a01c63c5e0d80`、heatmap checkpoint SHA256 `64b120afde4579563d8c18f4430eeaab00c3829adaa682fa82f321fac496ea7a`。数据为既有三条同源 Direct-HDR raw-event/GT robot trajectory，五个因果时间 bin、100 Hz poll；1/2 kHz 只改变事件采样 cadence，标签只在估计后离线计算。该输入共享理想 DvsEmulator 像素响应，因此本结果是采样充分性诊断，不是两个像素模型的比较。

| Episode | 1/2 kHz close action 一致率 | 闭合触发时差 | TTC 差 P95 | XY 状态差 P95 | 接触前 XY 误差 P95（1/2 kHz） | 接触前速度误差 P95（1/2 kHz） |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 000 | 93.8% | 50 ms | 517 ms | 40.70 mm | 10.53 / 10.91 mm | 0.207 / 0.218 m/s |
| 001 | 98.5% | 0 ms | 247 ms | 25.94 mm | 8.44 / 9.95 mm | 0.242 / 0.278 m/s |
| 002 | 97.5% | 0 ms | 129 ms | 6.85 mm | 7.77 / 8.09 mm | 0.261 / 0.242 m/s |

接触前 TTC 误差绝对 P95 在 1/2 kHz 下分别为：episode 000 `490/390 ms`、001 `517/478 ms`、002 `279/352 ms`；不宜只看 paired median 或图像 cosine 来冻结 cadence。episode 000 全段 fresh fraction 为 `54.6%`，另两条为 `100%`；三条接触前 XY 已接近厘米级，但速度/TTC 与 episode 000 的动作触发仍有明显差异。故**当前证据不足以冻结 1 或 2 kHz**；后续最小 paired smoke 暂用已有 2 kHz 决策输入，正式训练协议应将采样率列为待冻结项，若它会改变动作需只对关键决策窗补采/重放 5 kHz，并以 action/TTC/到达误差而非 cosine 评估。

该次离线回放 wall/user/system `12.10/10.91/0.55 s`、max RSS `394,656 KB`、exit 0。summary SHA256 `beba90f3f7f252529e6b41aa43d6dcbf95313aac767db177fa92e453bd653371`、逐 poll CSV `534e90bc0ead63130cfb52a57c44bea946a1a9e8c58cc239cf7de712467d2e7e`、目录 `SHA256SUMS` `dbbcf611121ae74bb65fef23386b6de234973138f161bae91dda4f98c084bb7b`；两处 raw-event NPZ 输入为 `8b7a69c0...`、`fe043e93...`、`50d5b246...`（完整值在 summary）。log/runtime/exit SHA256 `f17a90477871cc6cf5d76b612d3b1e4b2c111ca48b1a777766234a2017add37a / 3f2446be3abbf28917dfad9a5e0ec0f2df41c234d46d5fd749229ea8919240d6 / 9a271f2a916b0b6ee6cecb2426f0b3206ef074578be55d9bc94f6f3fe3ab86aa`。结果目录 [`r0_8_five_bin_sampling_20261017b/`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r0_8_five_bin_sampling_20261017b/) 已逐项通过 SHA256 核验。

#### R0.8.9：同缓存 rollout 的 Ours/V2E R1 数据与训练 smoke

g1 运行代码 commit `e13b0b307c464e3f753cd3bacaeb092ee5e22586`，scene SHA256 `aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a`、shared-control SHA256 `3d289c2bef1c562b26f9b713c2a826b813a286a1f7e21f9ead0a01c63c5e0d80`，seed `2026104130/31`，速度均 `0.4 m/s`、初始 y=`−55/+55 mm`。R1 smoke status PASS 仅表示 pipeline contract 完整：每集只跑一次物理/机器人 rollout，两生成器 hash 相同的 HDF5 scene cache、100 Hz frame timestamps、GT labels 和 episode-level split；训练 episode 0、validation episode 1，split contract SHA256 `8070763b5b2e98ecc6be76b054d4d917094f1a077c7440933d889649ecad509f`。cache HDF5 SHA256 `d72a0c86bdf1c4491b4c2ece68c500c10f23b9ccee1102919ae8c4d319124357 / a2c4d19bf953b6082f517a15da526e1e6247e663d698626e58db0d040655a72d`；frame timestamp SHA256 `d3f2d9416c05453473a75fa2740af552ab0ab1a9f93d70f53aaf484b9b96e77f / 8d34f211646dbb13062a01968722e7d9d7eda11cbbe0640913213ba63b81e2c9`。两份训练 source manifest SHA256 为 Ours `77e0adaec8c74f436a374e03231acfac4c0b7a4267e13a0d1de0f5b6820dada8`、V2E `c48535c54fb703af92f7dcb483ff87ae87cf2ed8729c37f105194def128f81ac`。

两模型各 fit 3 epochs，validation 各 208 个 causal five-bin 窗口、有效状态率 `100%`、状态 age P95 `10 ms`。Ours validation XY P95 `152.7 mm`、arrival P95 `328 ms`；V2E XY P95 `219.1 mm`、arrival P95 `841 ms`。样本只有一个训练 episode、一个横向 held-out episode，这些误差只说明最小训练/评价调用链运行，不代表泛化或生成器优劣。

两集机器人任务都不是 strict success：首接触 `0.6125/0.6235 s`，双侧接触 `0.6175/0.6270 s`，lift 命令 `1.106/1.115 s` 后共同 30 µm gate 报 `grasp_gate_abort`；第一集合格 hold `0`，第二集 `0.462 s`，未达到 strict 连续 `0.5 s`。strict scorer 仍是 `r0_5_strict_continuous_contact_clearance_slip`，没有改分数来通过 R1。

R1 训练生成器的独立性边界：Ours 使用缓存线性 HDR 和 GenesisPhysicsEventPlugin，理想 DVS 阈值 `0.2`、无噪声/无限带宽；官方 V2E 使用同时间戳 display RGB、SuperSloMo 与冻结 `v2e.yaml`（sigma `0.03`、cutoff `300 Hz`、leak/shot noise、refractory `0.5 ms`）。因此两训练组**共享 rollout/标签/split，但不共享 pixel-response model**；下游差异不能只解释为插帧/时间重建效果。两 R2 使用共同 Direct-HDR ideal-DVS event source，绕过 Ours/V2E 训练生成器，作为控制器接线的独立评估 smoke。

R1 wrapper 完整 wall/user/system/max RSS `20:06.87 / 1580.95 / 38.56 s / 2,536,068 KB`；阶段计时为共享机器人 rollout/cache `37.54 s`、两生成器合计 `1060.26 s`、两次 fit 合计 `41.35 s`。exit 0。摘要 SHA256 `3535a312a35111d5e0082a7116b0d564900b0e1154291501ae45eda22ce171ce`、cache manifest `f7b7c1b26a405a6603f765ca0b0255a3f9e821086b050968a0ab0b876c7be513`、g1 全结果 `SHA256SUMS` `cc2bb9939295ca0c9c34de61ea54d640207a9ad28a0c5e4a186ebfe8b0e671b9`；训练 checkpoint SHA256 Ours `0b1199d11ec22da4443bd0236e1852f6c23e90dd5a77067afd9e5517817277f5`、V2E `64c89a0e22974f3db3fe7fe34a98b3a013bacbabe3c5db013547ffff389b39a4`。全量 g1 输出（489 文件）保留于 `g1:/home/zhaoyuhan/Eventbased_WAM/repositories/genesis-event-camera-plugin-r0_8-final-oracle-20261017/benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r0_8_paired_pipeline_smoke_20261017c/`；版本库收录 64 文件/约 103 MB 可复核子集，portable manifest SHA256 `f77522d5dd76511110223c3c4ab2b3280b2aff51bfbdb9cbebccd0eddb016104` 全项通过，未复制 HDR/RGB/depth/seg frame cache 与 HDF5 cache；地址、遗漏范围与复跑方式见该结果目录 README。

#### R0.8.10：两个 checkpoint 的同源 Direct-HDR R2 新 episode smoke

两次均运行代码 commit `e13b0b307c464e3f753cd3bacaeb092ee5e22586`、同 scene/control hash、seed `2026104400`、`0.4 m/s`、中心 y、0.5 ms physics、2 kHz causal five-bin event controller；只替换 R1 fit checkpoint。两组 inference 只读 causal event state；proprioceptive grasp/lift gate 两组共用，GT/segmentation/depth 只进离线评估。

| checkpoint / runtime | 结果 | 关键决策误差 | 运行成本 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Ours `0b1199d11ec22da4443bd0236e1852f6c23e90dd5a77067afd9e5517817277f5` | 0 contact / 0 lift / strict fail；`failure_primary=oracle_unreachable` | close 在 `0.120 s`，固定 predictor 的自由滑动到达 `0.6544 s`；close 时估计 TTC `84.1 ms`、同一 predictor 的 GT-current TTC `534.6 ms`，误差 `−450.5 ms`；close 提前到达参考 `534.4 ms`。1/2 kHz XY error P95 `189.6/322.9 mm` | episode loop `234.88 s`；完整 Python wall `5:09.88`、user/system `277.98/23.33 s`、max RSS `1,761,380 KB` |
| V2E `64c89a0e22974f3db3fe7fe34a98b3a013bacbabe3c5db013547ffff389b39a4` | 首碰 `0.5725 s`，但无双侧接触、无 lift、strict fail；`failure_primary=unstable_grasp` | close 在 `0.110 s`，比自由滑动到达早 `544.4 ms`；预测 TTC `76.9 ms` 对 GT-current TTC `534.6 ms`，误差约 `−457.7 ms`；接触净开口 `11.684 mm`，低于 gate `12 mm` 行程门槛。1/2 kHz XY error P95 `160.7/136.6 mm` | episode loop `154.18 s`；完整 Python wall `3:42.67`、user/system `203.80/17.36 s`、max RSS `1,698,604 KB` |

R2 两组均 `0/1 strict success`，是独立 held-out episode 上的模型接线与失败定位 smoke，不是正式比较。Ours/V2E full result manifests 和 per-episode trace/GIF 已纳入结果目录； summary SHA256 `14b7b89fef5bc2ef9165dfb35336edfcb9790e083d38b9b7354b9742b05db7e7 / 3fe259654836c4d3dd0461d57b1a054479f23b2a930f9b61efc2ab0338d35fc4`，完整目录 SHA256SUMS 文件 `7ce23a2046eb39a8d37f2dd3bf745ff5d89b493f1f16ba1312796c02d9073e04 / 4d385dd4e3c07024bdb9437e2bd6415c1967b2494c93a1075ba6119ea0d3d3d2`。

**两个工作包当前结论：**R1 的同缓存配对/哈希/split/小预算 fit 调用链通过，R2 共同事件源/状态接口/控制/strict scorer 链路贯通但新 checkpoint 未闭环成功。采样对照仍不支持冻结 1 kHz；当前可暂用 2 kHz 作为开发默认（它匹配 0.5 ms 物理时钟），但必须保留 1/2 kHz 会改变部分动作的证据，不能写成采样已充分。训练像素响应不匹配、monitor false abort 与小数据造成的 TTC 泛化仍是正式实验前需在协议中明示的限制。

#### R0.8.11：60 µm gate 的 matched dynamic counterfactual（g1，2026-10-17）

运行代码 commit e13b0b307c464e3f753cd3bacaeb092ee5e22586，frozen simulator core e79e656ac00f7847b98db9e12bf43b093f3afff1。Genesis 1.2.2、Python 3.11.15、Torch 2.8.0 CPU physics、0.5 ms physics step；渲染使用 EGL GPU 3。场景 SHA256 aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a。诊断控制配置在有效控制参数上仅将 shared-control full-lift joint-drift monitor 从 30 µm 改为 60 µm；schema/provenance 同时标记为 diagnostic-only，SHA256 59ef2b10b4fd74f06aa35fbd0303448d28b32e0b63798d27bcc7d55bf2f76c42；30 µm 主控制配置 SHA256 3d289c2bef1c562b26f9b713c2a826b813a286a1f7e21f9ead0a01c63c5e0d80。闭合时序、机械参数、任务和 r0_5_strict_continuous_contact_clearance_slip scorer 均未改，旧评分仍单独保留。

命令（g1 隔离 worktree）：

~~~bash
cd /home/zhaoyuhan/Eventbased_WAM/repositories/genesis-event-camera-plugin-r0_8-final-oracle-20261017
bash benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/runtime_logs/r0_8_monitor60_matched_abort_counterfactual_20261017a.launch.sh
~~~

4 集为先前 30 µm 回归中的三个 abort seed 加一个 .5 m/s 左偏成功对照，非独立新样本；每档各 2 集。60 µm 下 strict success 2/4，按速度均为 1/2，Wilson 95% 区间各为 [0.095, 0.905]。逐集配对如下：

| seed | 速度 / 初始 y | 30 µm hold 与结果 | 60 µm hold 与结果 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2026104344 | 0.4 m/s / 0 mm | 0.473 s，gate abort，strict fail | 原 2/4 strict score 保留；1.7395 s monitor abort 时 hold 0.4715 s（尚未完成），episode 在 abort 后继续到 1.768 s 时 scorer 累计值才到 0.5 s |
| 2026104345 | 0.4 m/s / +55 mm | 0.1425 s，gate abort | 0.1715 s，gate abort，strict fail |
| 2026104346 | 0.5 m/s / −55 mm | 0.500 s，strict success | 0.500 s，strict success |
| 2026104347 | 0.5 m/s / 0 mm | 0 s，gate abort | 0 s，gate abort，strict fail |

seed 4344 的 60 µm 结果中，monitor 于 1.7395 s abort，届时 qualified hold 为 0.4715 s，**尚未满足连续 0.5 s**；episode 在 abort 后继续，至 1.768 s 原 scorer 的累计记录才到 0.5 s。按研究会要求保留原 2/4 strict score，但不得表述为 hold 先完成再 abort。按已合格 hold 窗计算的真实最小底面净空为 80.079 mm、最大相对滑移 4.339 mm，clearance 达标时支撑接触样本为 0；abort 后至 episode 终点的评分时间语义列为 R2 诊断问题。seed 4345 的关节漂移为 63.021 µm，qualified hold 内最大滑移 3.754 mm，但只有 0.1715 s；seed 4347 最大净空仅 35.263 mm，未进入 qualified hold。其余闭合提前量和实际接触状态与 30 µm 配对轨迹相符，没有重调闭合机械参数。

这项小样本把一个仅差 27 ms 的中心 hold 从失败变为 strict success，但没有挽救 .4 m/s +55 mm 或 .5 m/s 中心样例；不能把 2/4 外推成稳定成功率。此前 60 µm 静态/侧偏负例回归通过，但没有覆盖所有动态前缘顶推轨迹。建议把 60 µm 作为下一版共同控制协议候选：它允许严格任务完成，且已有负例 smoke 未见 false success；正式冻结前仍应由研究会话决定是否接受尚未覆盖的动态前缘风险。30 µm 结果保留为保守对照，不合并统计。

逐集数据、trace 和成功/失败 GIF 在本地 [r0_8_monitor60_matched_abort_counterfactual_20261017a](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r0_8_monitor60_matched_abort_counterfactual_20261017a/)，g1 原始目录为 /home/zhaoyuhan/Eventbased_WAM/repositories/genesis-event-camera-plugin-r0_8-final-oracle-20261017/benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r0_8_monitor60_matched_abort_counterfactual_20261017a/（约 13.9 MB）。本地 SHA256SUMS 六项均通过。CSV SHA256 d284a218f0b79afd819bc5380168ae36cd9fe4eee87ef31a7a81ab2d55d95d86；summary 00ff59ae4f570499bf15f93423ff48d5934628f30b2e4f82c0da64f393018941；manifest 44978878994202e503bc243753f755d2aca79d9c00be2596873fce6f28d2f93b；目录 SHA256SUMS 文件 3bd56357cd5ce0c953a9e78cb1b4932f017a5f7485047cc4d8478198baeac64b。启动器 SHA256 9bdb3d98bf5e58535aebf9fe2e42c385cd9aebc161ac0e0eaf612af61ab8048a；log/runtime/exit/pid SHA256 890787fa36ea4aedc1cc0bc8854e053bce863c321a9afd587445f91793d10943 / 45da82522ff4e6dbb1572f9f83fbebd97c585c224b319134c40ae1260fd78f2b / 9a271f2a916b0b6ee6cecb2426f0b3206ef074578be55d9bc94f6f3fe3ab86aa / 952d5d75d08c8a2f942c64782687eeaaa232d8f0cce7fc4e34c54479f5f127f5。wall/user/system/max RSS 为 154.33/147.04/5.41 s/1,493,496 KB，exit 0。运行日志有共享 Quadrants cache lock warning；没有清理 cache 或干预其他用户进程。

#### 两个工作包的协议候选与边界

这是 2026-10-17 的协议候选快照：建议场景/机器人/机位/0.5 ms physics/strict scorer 按既有 hash 锁定，controller 保留 80/70/60 ms close lead，lift gate 暂提 60 µm。其“不匹配 pixel-response、只可作最小流水线起点”的 R1 判断已由 2026-10-18 small9 共同响应开发批次更新；采样与 gate 的冻结问题仍属 R2/协议审查。


#### R1 优先执行（2026-10-17 至 18；最小开发评估已完成，正式测试未启动）

研究会已在 Windows 研究工作区 docs/robot_r1_priority_2026-10-17/ 核验旧 GT-shadow 的共同参考回放：9 集、每模型 581 个接触前查询、平均平面位置误差约 95 mm。该回放使用同一 Direct-HDR 文件/时刻，说明共同 evaluator 可复用；旧 checkpoint 训练仅一个 episode × 3 epochs，且 Ours/V2E 训练 pixel response 不一致，不能做生成器排名。参考集只作开发，不作为正式独立测试。

本轮执行要求：在不修改 frozen simulator core 的前提下，为 Ours 和官方 V2E 使用明确命名的共同理想无噪声响应配置；保留官方默认配置和旧结果；以相同模型、episode split、训练预算及训练 seed 训练配对模型；两组模型统一在相同旧 Direct-HDR 文件与查询时刻上评估位置、速度、固定预测器到达时间偏差、拦截横向偏差及状态/可达覆盖率。R1 不等待 R2 抓取成功或夹爪 monitor 收敛。结果见下节。

研究证据入口：`/mnt/c/Users/zhaoyuhan/Documents/ChatGPT/New project/docs/robot_r1_priority_2026-10-17/`。其中 `R1_PRIORITY_REVIEW.md` 与 `R1_EXECUTION_PROMPT.md` 为研究会说明；`common_reference_audit.json`、`common_reference_polls.csv`、`run_common_reference_audit.py` 为此前回放证据。R1 small9 的代码/配置/checkpoint/事件/查询时刻/data hashes、复跑命令、episode/速度指标及端到端成本已列在下节。

#### R1 共同响应实现历程（已提交并在 g1 完成九集开发批次）

代码已推送到 `exp/robot-closed-loop-v1`，commit `10ff100caf5e79f9f8a75b575b080f3cb01c10b4`。新增缓存回放训练入口 `benchmark/robot_closed_loop/diagnostics/run_r1_from_cached_rollouts.py`、共同 Direct-HDR 评估入口 `benchmark/robot_closed_loop/diagnostics/evaluate_r1_common_direct_hdr.py`，并让官方 V2E wrapper 可接收独立实验 YAML。Python 编译和 `git diff --check` 通过；此处仅为静态检查，不代表 R1 已运行。

共同响应配置 [`r1_shared_ideal_response_v1.json`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/configs/r1_shared_ideal_response_v1.json) SHA256 `4b5bd964835b9b09559a12b127779bc05d371b68f1dc6a2e0d231d148c953a72`；V2E 独立 overlay [`r1_shared_ideal_response_v1_v2e.yaml`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/configs/r1_shared_ideal_response_v1_v2e.yaml) SHA256 `8abe329a0013c5cd47dd3050576656cacfa9b15e487c6e8f8a1b9ff6d7ac1505`。官方默认 [`v2e.yaml`](../../benchmark/configs/v2e.yaml) 未修改，SHA256 `155f0ef7305bac9c52aa09e485d4c5a1feeaf448f1279a2cccf04097b290add4`。两训练路径从相同缓存 PNG 由 OpenCV 读出灰度端点，使用相同 V2E-compatible `lin_log`、ON/OFF 阈值 `0.2`、零阈值扰动、零噪声/泄漏/不应期和无限带宽；Ours 几何重建继续使用缓存深度、分割和物体/相机位姿，V2E 继续走官方 SuperSloMo。缓存 HDR 仍纳入 rollout HDF5 hash，但不冒称为此次 Ours 输入的 scene-linear radiance。时间重建路径保持不同；该共同配置只统一可控像素响应，不声称两生成算法逐事件相同。

最小缓存开发预算已写入实验配置：同一 `heatmap_v2`、seed `606`、固定最多 `240` epochs（patience 同为 240，验证集选 checkpoint）、batch `32`；旧两集按 episode 拆为 episode 0 train、episode 1 validation。训练集仅一个 episode，所以该步只验证共同响应下的端到端接线与开发评估，不足以判断模型是否学会跨速度/横向泛化。之后根据精度和成本再扩至三速度 × 中心/左右的小批；现有九集 Direct-HDR 参考全部是已查看的开发材料，不能充当正式测试集。

新评估脚本会为每个 episode 重建 estimator，避免速度回归历史跨 episode 串入；两组在完全相同的 Direct-HDR 文件与 query 时刻上回放，并输出逐 poll/逐 episode/逐速度的 XY 位置误差、XY 速度误差、固定 predictor 到达时间与拦截位置误差，以及缺失状态和预测可达/不可达判断覆盖率。到达/拦截参考是同一 fixed predictor 输入评估时刻 GT 的结果，不是实际过线真值。

此前的二集调用链 smoke 和旧 checkpoint 回放继续按历史结果保留，不升级为本轮验收。本轮先有一次 launcher 因过宽 warning 正则把合法 V2E 日志中的时间戳误判为 300 Hz 告警，随后修复到 `123369f4bd70a1fe039bf073a35333d0d30ead19`；失败尝试生成的事件和 checkpoint 未用于本轮训练/评估。最终 small9 运行从同一不可变九集 cache 重新生成两组训练事件，launcher exit `0`，完整结果和逐查询 evaluator 输出见下方 R1 small9 closeout。

合计 Ours reachable `384/581 (66.1%)`、V2E `514/581 (88.5%)`；state missing `0/581`。所有四类指标均同时输出 mean/p50/P95 和各自有效样本数；arrival/intercept 只在预测为 reachable 的 query 上可计算。特别是 Ours 的正 y 边缘三个独立参考 episode（`y=+55 mm`，速度 0.30/0.40/0.50）均出现明显发散：位置 mean `125–128 mm`、reach coverage `41–51%`；中心样本明显好于该边缘。V2E 本小批没有相同退化。该模式说明 Ours 尚未对共同 reference 域稳定；样本不足以判断是时间重建/event distribution 差异、模型容量/预算还是两者交互，不能称生成器胜负或转入正式排名。接触前最后 100 ms 的补充分层、episode 级全指标与逐 poll 原始 CSV 一并提交，避免只看均值/可达子集。

接触前最后 100 ms（按评估侧 GT 首接触时刻离线切片，不作为输入）的 Ours/V2E 位置误差 mean/P95 为 `37.5/185.2` 与 `6.6/14.0 mm`，速度误差 `0.894/3.493` 与 `0.162/0.365 m/s`，拦截 XY 误差 `15.8/52.5` 与 `9.0/30.8 mm`，reachable coverage 为 `49/90 (54.4%)` 与 `82/90 (91.1%)`；同固定 predictor 的绝对 TTC 偏差为 `71.1/185.1` 与 `78.2/317.7 ms`。TTC 只在各自判为 reachable 的 query 计算，故同时列出 coverage，避免将其单独解释为质量排序。

#### R1 原生 HDR 输入恢复与失败链路收尾（g1，2026-10-19）

本阶段沿用 `exp/robot-closed-loop-v1`，保留 `7fb72dc` 已审计批次和全部旧结果；使用隔离 g1 worktree `/home/zhaoyuhan/Eventbased_WAM/repositories/genesis-event-camera-plugin-r1-nativehdr-20261019a`，没有覆盖后续提交。长任务实际运行代码 commit `4c5f5f29d4c46dd50401c0aa9079874f97afcbc6`，冻结 simulator core 未修改。

**输入路径已核实并恢复到 native HDR 候选：**旧 Ours 配对训练使用缓存 display PNG 的 OpenCV grayscale + `video_linlog`，不是 native HDR。新 Ours 事件读取 `trajectory_cache.h5/radiance`（Genesis `camera.render(radiance=True)` 的 float32 scene-linear renderer radiance），走插件 `physical_log`：亮度自然对数、`1e-6` floor、gain 1、ON/OFF threshold 0.2，无噪声/滤波/refractory。HDR 数值不是显示灰度；renderer 未提供绝对物理辐射标定，阈值也未声称按 EVK4 实物校准。V2E 保留官方 display-video + SuperSloMo 路径，训练事件从已审计来源逐字节复用；没有后处理删事件。深度/分割/位姿只给 Ours 生成器内部重建用，模型输入仍只有因果五-bin 事件张量。两组事件响应/重建路径仍不同，以下只能作开发诊断。

**最先出错的位置：网络定位。**在已知 +55 mm 失败截点，旧 Ours checkpoint 的 Direct-HDR XY 定位误差为 185.8 mm，中心对照为 16.3 mm；之后的 50 ms 历史 OLS 易被位置跳变放大。精确状态重渲染关键帧与原参考像素一致；短窗离线密集 HDR 重放的 log-radiance 误差很小（+55 目标区域 mean 0.00700、背景 0.00218），unknown/hold 占比平均 0.00143%。dense replay 来自缓存精确状态，不是新动力学、事件真值或插值冒充 direct render。

确认 raw Ours events 在 100 Hz source frame seam 有明显 burst：+55 mm seam ±1 ms 为 31,116 events，等长帧间窗口 1,501（20.73×）；中心为 33,381/1,346（24.80×）。时间/坐标/极性/five-bin 转换已核对。**帧边界聚集已证实，是否为定位误差的原因、责任是否在 frozen plugin 内仍未证实。**同一旧 checkpoint +55 截点上网络 velocity head error 0.006 m/s，历史 OLS 2.625 m/s、pairwise median 2.167 m/s；整段 58 polls 对应 mean 0.060、2.037、1.852 m/s。但位置误差仍 185.8 mm。V2E 的速度头在相同对照中并不优于其历史估速，因此不应只给 Ours 改接口。详见[失败链诊断报告与代表图](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_failure_chain_diagnosis_20261019a/README.md)。

**原生 HDR 小规模重训已完成，但共同参考仍不通过。**九集 Ours 生成 12,323,507 events；保持 `heatmap_v2`、seed 606、最多 40 epochs、batch 32、episode split `[0,1,2,3,7,8]/[4,5,6]`。两组各有 566 own-domain validation windows；Ours native HDR validation XY P95 38.8 mm。可是同一 581 poll、已看过的 Direct-HDR 开发参考上，新 Ours checkpoint 定位 P95 444.9 mm、可达覆盖 315/581（54.2%）；V2E 为 11.3 mm、514/581（88.5%）。特别是 +55 mm 三档仍有明显定位错误。这说明恢复 HDR 输入和等预算重训仍不足以恢复 Ours 对共同参考域的能力；不能称为生成器排名，因为两组像素响应/重建路径不同，测试也是旧开发集。状态均 fresh `581/581`；该参考没有 GT-unreachable query，因此无法估计正确拒绝率。arrival/intercept 仅是同一冻结 predictor 对查询 cutoff GT state 的输出差，不是实测过线时间。

| 速度 | Ours native HDR：可达覆盖 / XY P95 / 速度 P95 | V2E：可达覆盖 / XY P95 / 速度 P95 |
|---:|---:|---:|
| 0.30 m/s | 140/275 (50.9%) / 428.3 mm / 7.532 m/s | 217/275 (78.9%) / 12.5 mm / 0.318 m/s |
| 0.40 m/s | 87/174 (50.0%) / 409.0 mm / 8.353 m/s | 165/174 (94.8%) / 11.2 mm / 0.279 m/s |
| 0.50 m/s | 88/132 (66.7%) / 460.7 mm / 7.257 m/s | 132/132 (100%) / 8.4 mm / 0.283 m/s |

运行配置 [`r1_native_hdr_small9_development_v1.json`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/configs/r1_native_hdr_small9_development_v1.json) SHA256 `351c09ae190f41e27ce2262cdf4bd5eaa932a96e685191ae9a309ffc3c1d5e1b`；scene SHA `aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a`，control SHA `3d289c2bef1c562b26f9b713c2a826b813a286a1f7e21f9ead0a01c63c5e0d80`，split SHA `0d45dbc930f76d73409f2b5d6b29f4676cb68c4394675039d8aac264cb7e6e56`，reference manifest SHA `86cf6c0801365c0c1e5949e4e04d3178fa03f334997db6d09547f69a2b5af37c`。Ours/V2E checkpoint SHA `6ecea6a4520ae09b8cc99ebfd8da7a6a324363d2e57dbf73790e27ee2dbec8d2 / a7140b63f3b5042b0dc0ed7cc3aa2974c4e9859db919f318826358883f7ce8a0`；共同 eval summary/poll CSV SHA `ae6db79403eb134ba79460225579a75ecd822e57b54f1be2b455ee8ed38aee4c / 8189910cd1eb7ddb17240eb0ab58e3a35f1b5ca2bc6c7f99a50fc62b0e7fa4c2`。Ours source manifest SHA `83b09e3e20fc86631da17336a73cc1ead2926a77eaac7618c3d76b48411714e8`，V2E 原 source manifest SHA `74d42e3c822ddfe12f5f154b8628a7b0054c0b7d98eb76da666443402c70ff0a`。每集 cache/input/event hashes 在 native retrain summary；g1 完整输出 `SHA256SUMS` 的 110 项均通过，其 manifest 文件 SHA256 为 `b7698cc2579d510aff5f353125dd9f3488b9c5109cb1ef65b7e19323c7a3fc5c`。

**当时的状态与建议：**HDR 输入转换、有限数值、事件坐标/极性/分箱检查未发现显示灰度误标或接口错误；native-HDR retrain 没恢复共同参考能力。该段提出的“固定 +55 mm 与中心缓存状态，比较插件事件与 direct-render”的下一步已由下面的 R1.5 实验完成，更新结论见文末；正式测试集/排名仍未启动。

#### R1 Ours 定位失效：采样频率与事件源诊断（2026-10-19，g1 已完成）

新诊断固定旧开发集中的 episode 004 中央对照与 episode 005 `+55 mm` 失败段，使用缓存物理状态窗口 `.5505–.5905 s`。首个启动前置检查发现旧状态 trace 没有保存所有 0.5 ms 物理状态（例如 `.5510 s` 缺失），脚本在渲染前拒绝了该查询；失败退出码 1，不产生任何诊断结果。为保持物理轨迹不被合成，修正为离线精确渲染窗口内**实际保存的所有状态行**，并报告真实、不均匀的时间间隔；对比稀疏 100 Hz HDR 端点经 frozen 插件重建、这些精确状态直接 HDR 亮度送入相同 `DvsEmulator` 响应，以及原归档 2 kHz Direct-HDR reference。首个完成的 pilot (`20261019b`) 显示强烈事件分布变化，但其 summary 把整集 archived raw-event 数与 40 ms 新生成片段放在同一 `raw_total` 列，口径不齐；保留在 g1 作历史诊断，不用于最终数量比较。最终 `20261019c` 已把三个来源先裁到相同 `.5505–.5905 s` 区间再统计。两个 checkpoint 在同一 `.5905 s` cutoff 上读取因果五-bin 输入。GT/分割/深度/位姿只用于 Ours 生成或推理后的评分，不送入模型；不推进物理，不更改 frozen core，不将合成状态称作 direct render。此项只分辨当前可用输入采样/重建与网络定位的关系，不作方法排名。

- 诊断代码初始提交 `ca6d8fb94c29b651ea5fc14dea23d1faf047945b`；最终 g1 执行代码 commit 为 `458295f62cbe396ee861cc5ebbacc0cbbfff9cb0`。第一次执行前置校验的 commit `1c2ba91a86b076625bf88bab66f439ebc6331684` 在请求不存在的 `.5510 s` 状态时按预期 fail-fast，退出码 1，尚未启动 Genesis/渲染。成功的 provisional pilot `20261019b` 保留在 g1，但其 archived raw-event 总数与 40 ms 新生成事件片段的统计分母不一致，不作为最终数量证据。最终 `20261019c` 已对三个事件源统一裁到 `.5505–.5905 s` 后统计。配置 [`r1_ours_localization_cadence_ablation_20261019c.json`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/configs/r1_ours_localization_cadence_ablation_20261019c.json) SHA256 `6845b34d6f22939dc5761c38cf82b0546ac70a907e78866131c05edf32171a1f`。新增稀疏/直接两条路径使用同一 physical-log 响应；归档参考仅在近黑像素 floor 约定 `log(luminance+1e-6)` vs `log(max(luminance,1e-6))` 不同。

- **最终结果：** 两集都使用同一 `.5905 s` 查询和同一因果五-bin 接口。中心 episode 004：Ours checkpoint 在稀疏 Ours / exact logged-state direct / archived 2 kHz 输入上的 XY 误差为 `10.0 / 79.6 / 54.1 mm`；V2E checkpoint 在完全相同输入上的误差为 `8.0 / 8.2 / 3.6 mm`。`+55 mm` episode 005 对应为 Ours `4.0 / 20.3 / 14.8 mm`，V2E `5.9 / 14.4 / 4.4 mm`。这是每集单查询的诊断值，不是误差分布或排名。两模型对每个事件源读取相同 tensor hash；所有六个模型/输入组合状态均 fresh、五个 bin 均有事件。
- +55 mm 的 Ours 100 Hz 路径在 seam `0.5605/0.5705/0.5805 s` 的 ±1 ms 窗分别有 `4,756/6,154/5,200` 个事件；帧间等宽窗口只有 `23/16/14` 个。其因果 25 ms 输入为 `15,278` events；exact logged-state direct 为 `2,888`，归档 2 kHz reference 为 `3,776`。五-bin tensor 与归档 reference cosine 分别为 `0.217`、`0.908`。中心 episode 也呈现同方向差异。它证实 Ours 稀疏重建事件存在边界突发和明显输入分布差异；两条路径也有采样密度差，尚不能把定位错误单因归结为 seam。
- **哪里最先出错、如何修：** 同一个相机/投影/标签下，V2E checkpoint 从同一 tensor 定位接近 GT，而 Ours checkpoint 仅在自己的稀疏事件上接近，说明当前证据更支持 Ours 权重对事件源分布敏感，非共享投影/标签换算问题。此前对原生 HDR 做条件匹配 9 集重训也未恢复共同 Direct-HDR 开发集性能。短期不应改控制器或 GT 预测器去补偿错误定位。首个候选修复方向是让 Ours 事件重建的时序采样/帧交界响应与目标评估输入匹配，再按相同 episode split 重训；但 burst 的具体根因尚未定位。若修复需要编辑 `genesis_event_plugin/` frozen interpolation/plugin，应先向研究会提交本复现和最小改动建议，获得方向审查后再碰核心。当前未做本轮重训。
- g1 使用 Genesis 1.2.2 / Torch 2.8.0+cpu；精确 trace 重渲染每集 47 帧，间隔中位 1 ms、最小 0.5 ms、有效均值约 1.15 kHz，不是完整 2 kHz trace，也没有合成缺失物理状态。没有推进物理、没有创建新 rollout。summary wall `84.005 s`；`/usr/bin/time` elapsed `91.13 s`、peak RSS `1,724,560 KB`。逐集 HDR render `0.99/1.25 s`、100 Hz plugin generation `2.37/3.49 s`、exact-row event emulation `0.45/0.55 s`。Genesis 共享 Quadrants cache lock 有警告但运行 exit 0；没有清理共享 cache。GPU 0 当时由其他用户作业占用且未触碰，诊断模型和事件计算用 CPU，仅把 GPU 1 暴露给 renderer。
- 可复核结果：[`R1 localization cadence report`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_ours_localization_cadence_ablation_20261019c/README.md)、逐来源模型 CSV、两 episode JSON/NPZ/图像、以及 `SHA256SUMS`。output manifest SHA256 `9aa4cffee12548aeb785a78826293e2deefca5397628a10173973f92456c4c83`；README SHA256 `94c7935d699599ec137df602654eff4e8e43b996f17f66b23e7137a03eb3d96f`；summary `c84b277be811bcc9ba083611608ea9fb10a89f8ed2e9302f5e0385c50c36c8b4`；CSV `837b3b7de8113c8d029ea7150a2d5551948f2038b3b2b504fd34d8f7930fda00`；runtime log SHA256 `e1142c68792e0c79b1c9879445832274d9006aad5fd9d3c8ffcb36c5492ca24e`；exit file 内容为 0。g1 portable tar SHA256 `fb0909b694314286e07a2ccfe1ac2d1bcec781dfe4096372a77583fe2e2c8d91`，已复制到仓库并验证所有输出 hash。
- 复跑命令与待归档输出根目录：

- g1 预检：指定的缓存 Direct-HDR manifest SHA256 与既有记录一致，为 `86cf6c0801365c0c1e5949e4e04d3178fa03f334997db6d09547f69a2b5af37c`；两集原始事件、两个 checkpoint 均存在。通过 `gec-robot/.venv-robot` 的 Genesis/PyTorch/dependency import；Genesis 1.2.2 对应 PyTorch `2.8.0+cpu`。conda 环境 PyTorch 2.5.1 会触发 Genesis 版本警告，因此未用于运行。临启动前 GPU 0 突然出现他人 PID `756955`（用户 `zyl` 的 `python train.py`，14.5 GiB、99% 利用率）；未触碰该进程。GPU 1 当时空闲（4 MiB、0%），故执行时将仅暴露 GPU 1 给 renderer，模型/事件计算仍显式使用 CPU。内存可用约 487 GiB；旧 R1 工作树保持原样。

- 复跑仍使用上面的 g1 命令并提供一个新的隔离输出目录名。结论目前适合提交研究会审阅的是：Ours 的错误由事件源变化触发的定位偏移，而高强度 100 Hz seam burst 是相关候选；需进一步做时序采样因果隔离后才能选择修复。该批次不表示机器人抓取性能、不作 Ours/V2E 排名，未修改 frozen core。

#### R1.5 事件生成一致性候选（2026-10-19；设计与启动前记录，结果见文末）

本阶段依据研究审查 `C:/Users/zhaoyuhan/Documents/ChatGPT/New project/docs/robot_r1_5_audit_2026-10-19/REVIEW.md` 及同目录审计 JSON/脚本推进。审查确认至少有两个独立生成问题；Ours 旧 checkpoint 对 0.585–0.595 s 的 0.5 ms 读取相位也高度敏感，不能只凭单个时刻评估。

**研究侧已有证据（未在本轮服务器复跑）：**episode 004/005 的真实端点缓存 alpha=1 时，source1 投影最大偏差分别为 `1.907e-6 / 3.052e-5 px`，冻结 NumPy warp 有 `343 / 613` 个可见像素 log 亮度误差超过 0.2。只将 source1 端点映射干预为精确恒等后，超过阈值像素归零且输出逐像素一致；静态重复帧误差为 0、事件为 0。这个同数据干预确认了端点数值问题的局部因果联系；“微小投影通过最近深度竞争排除主要像素贡献”仍需候选缓存运行中进一步检视。中间真实运动没有取整。

静止平面 log 亮度 10 ms 内从 0 平滑增加至 0.5 的控制中，`next_primary` 第一张 1 ms 子帧就采用末端亮度，造成事件由正确的 `4/8 ms` 提前到 `0.4/0.8 ms`；像素模型直接读取平滑帧仍为 `4/8 ms`。这证明时间亮度处理存在局限，不代表机器人 seam 的唯一原因。研究侧对完整 archived Direct-HDR 输入作了 21 个时刻的相位诊断：Ours 误差范围中心 `20.2–204.5 mm`、+55 mm `13.9–170.0 mm`；V2E 分别约 `1.0–7.4 / 1.8–7.5 mm`。这些是旧 checkpoint 的传递诊断，不是修复成绩。

**新实现仅在 benchmark diagnostics，frozen core 未改：**

- [`r1_5_consistency_candidates.py`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/diagnostics/r1_5_consistency_candidates.py) 分离精确端点返回、source1 精确身份投影，以及 log-radiance 区间候选；中间投影保持连续，不取整。亮度候选仅在两端同标签、深度一致且可见的区域插值，其余像素显式请求 direct render。
- [`diagnose_r1_5_consistency.py`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/diagnostics/diagnose_r1_5_consistency.py) 复用既有中心/+55 mm HDR 源缓存、reference、checkpoint 和固定 scene/control。对 trace 中真实存在的中间状态行做离线 direct render，不推进物理、不造状态。对照原 100 Hz 插件、同状态行冻结 warp、仅 source1 身份投影、准确端点、带真实请求 render fill 的光度候选、exact-state direct-render 与 archived 2 kHz reference；报告图像分区误差、mask/成本、raw events、five-bin 及两模型在 `.585–.595 s` 每 `0.5 ms` 的定位。端点重渲染还会和已哈希缓存逐像素比对。
- log-linear 亮度变化只是待证候选，不能自动代表真实阴影或遮挡。未知区域填充使用相同 exact trace state 的缓存/真实渲染图，并分别统计请求比例与完整帧渲染成本。GT 只用于生成几何和推理后评分，不输入网络。

**已实测：**代码 commit `c427e574922aa5c9676151c3404437a74dce9f75` 的 NumPy `--analytic-control`。静态最大亮度误差 `0`、事件 `0`；10 ms ramp 首末 log 亮度 `0.05/0.50`，候选/直接输入均在 `4/8 ms` 产生事件、各 `512` 个；合成 alpha=1 投影身份和端点返回最大误差各为 `0`。中间合成物体运动最大 `2.156 px`，候选与冻结投影差为 `0 px`。这不是 episode 缓存通过结论，也未覆盖 Torch/CUDA。

小型结果入口：[`R1.5 analytic controls`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_candidate_controls_20261019a/README.md)。解析输出 SHA256 `d4b7df4333a2e76d3076a82fcbd7fe3cfdf86c5db5b37eec67403afceef590e2`；目录 manifest SHA256 `c037fbf3e6e155207b143bb4a5360952f5df5df41728d96828829826efe53f69`。候选实现 commit `c427e574922aa5c9676151c3404437a74dce9f75`；缓存 runner 成本/端点检查补充 commit `f9c54cfbfed6a8c52c3a788274c66713935e9ede`。候选 config/module/cache runner SHA256 分别 `13efc23498f82c9e0f27539c06abc4554b831a9173ca5e8d5fc2bd603a326226 / 98ba537df324133fb9b93faba8f5f83a42f74161aab88fb6dfa1e4e26996a5cc / 3547d2f07ccdbbdd6a4c047cb45b5d8bb754f94e670e9d82725d6925d57b8955`。scene/control、Ours/V2E checkpoint SHA 沿用本节上游记录：`aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a / 3d289c2bef1c562b26f9b713c2a826b813a286a1f7e21f9ead0a01c63c5e0d80` 与 `6ecea6a4520ae09b8cc99ebfd8da7a6a324363d2e57dbf73790e27ee2dbec8d2 / a7140b63f3b5042b0dc0ed7cc3aa2974c4e9859db919f318826358883f7ce8a0`。原 reference manifest SHA256 `86cf6c0801365c0c1e5949e4e04d3178fa03f334997db6d09547f69a2b5af37c`；中心/+55 mm source cache SHA256 `0525c18c8d89f5831ef0cb71ac9e3b6b130d60579daf374f06d5cfcee5b879d7 / 91c1397642e317d6bb51150b4107c518b52e4fa9fcf5f1caf6a2a91315c7d434`。

本节下方原有“尚未完成”清单和启动命令记录的是执行前状态，现已由文末“R1.5 实测收敛状态”中的 g1 实测结果、哈希和复跑命令取代。此前的解析控制数据仍作为历史证据保留。

#### R1.5 实测收敛状态（2026-10-19；g1 已完成）

场景、相机、机器人任务、控制器和 strict scorer 沿用既有配置；研究审查指定的中心 episode 004 与 +55 mm episode 005 作为开发诊断，不作独立测试或排名。所有事件/模型对照复用同一缓存物理状态序列和相机；离线精确状态渲染不推进物理，也不生成缺失的中间 GT 状态。

**已确认的问题与影响：**

- 在真实缓存的精确端点，NumPy 最大投影偏差约 `1.5e-5 / 6.1e-5 px`、Torch CPU 约 `7.6e-5 px`，但最近深度 z-buffer / 可见性竞争会把微小坐标误差放大成大量不同像素。精确 source identity 端点候选让端点画面逐像素恢复。它只处理数学上的精确端点，不对中间物体运动取整。
- 平滑亮度控制证实 `next_primary` 会在区间第一个生成子帧立即采用全部末端亮度：10 ms ramp 的阈值事件从应有的 `4/8 ms` 提前到 `0.4/0.8 ms`。静态图仍为零事件。这证明亮度时间处理有局限，但不单独证明真实轨迹中的全部 seam 来源。
- exact-row cadence sweep 显示事件量随输入端点频率急剧上升。40 ms 对照内，100/200/500/1000 Hz warp 路径在 center 产生 `23,690/53,177/133,592/259,960` events，在 +55 mm 产生 `24,776/45,090/111,557/218,197`；直接将窗口内已保存的精确状态 HDR 画面送入同一个 DVS 响应仅有 `2,807/4,618` events。raw event 数量级明显不一致。
- 只把每个区间最后的 `alpha=1` 重建帧换成其精确端点 luminance，保持所有中间子帧原样，事件量下降 `17.5–30.1%`，但剩余仍比 exact-state direct-render 高 `7–76×`。因此端点投影 bug 是真实且有贡献的数值错误，但不是事件量膨胀和方块定位失败的唯一原因。
- 端点候选在单个 `.5905 s` cutoff 的模型误差变化不一致，不能说明定位已修复。更完整的 21 个 query phase 中，现有 Ours checkpoint 在 exact-state direct-render 输入的 XY P95 为 center `127.8 mm`、+55 mm `167.1 mm`；端点候选 P95 也随模型/episode 大幅变化。原生 HDR 模型在当前 Direct-HDR 共同参考上的域适配仍失败。
- 通过实际 render fills 的 log-radiance 区间候选能降低目标区域图像误差，某些查询更准，但每集 `53/59` 个真实状态行都至少请求一次 direct render，约覆盖累计图像像素的 `9.3–9.4%`；该成本尚不足以支持它作为正式在线路径，事件/模型结果也不稳定。

**结果、配置和成本：**

- Exact-state image/event replay 与全部 21 个 query phases：[`r1_5_consistency_candidates_20261019b_realrender_g1`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_consistency_candidates_20261019b_realrender_g1/)，输出 manifest 文件 SHA256 `49764b3f4e70b406c34d1c41d46ea344c13b9b9cb8d2ca85fd4404563c69ae15`。执行代码 commit `2690935cac25a943148201bb66c31af63d65fd23`，配置 SHA256 `13efc23498f82c9e0f27539c06abc4554b831a9173ca5e8d5fc2bd603a326226`，总 wall `197.931 s`；118 次精确状态 render 共 `3.763 s`，exact direct event emulation 共 `1.433 s`。
- Exact-row cadence sweep：[`r1_ours_localization_cadence_sweep_20261019d`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_ours_localization_cadence_sweep_20261019d/)，manifest SHA256 `010ef8dda4351b4b6f10ead6f4e8d3dd3b0edd837ef9881a24c73e63c144959b`。执行代码 commit `2690935cac25a943148201bb66c31af63d65fd23`，配置 SHA256 `9b4f0c84ca2ec2615b2a3de7f6500e8b3ece9189a18989c15c036896f66bd329`；40 ms clip 的实际 trace 有 47 行/episode，间隔 min `0.5 ms`、median/max `1 ms`，有效均值约 `1.15 kHz`。两集 render wall 共 `2.860 s`，全诊断 Python wall `187.398 s`。200/500/1000 Hz 采样只挑真实保存状态，不合成状态。
- 端点单因素 cadence isolation：[`r1_5_endpoint_terminal_cadence_isolation_20261019e`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_endpoint_terminal_cadence_isolation_20261019e/)，manifest 文件 SHA256 `cca6858a08ff3ed1ee1c0d48c8e50a81c6e323c7e98e6394969256024b0cfdaf`。执行代码 commit `2d782fbd75c7dc3d64b39e6af32364caa5e2f795`，配置 [`r1_5_endpoint_terminal_cadence_isolation_20261019e.json`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/configs/r1_5_endpoint_terminal_cadence_isolation_20261019e.json) SHA256 `8fdd5e668ae6c2316ac9ad4449511c1f9fef14d7c1ce28b2a98874931f951f3b`；total Python wall `289.005 s`，render wall `3.061 s`，模型/事件计算使用 CPU、GPU0 用于 headless renderer。runtime log SHA256 `6b64a389e6736f00ac4d4d14eccac6965bf42762535ef206a947283ba020cc34`，exit 文件内容为 `0`；launcher SHA256 `4e09c8dd10879c99f3cc8063561f86271f67fbc455fc7739e9ed29fc6b44f5a1`。
- 固定参数沿用 scene SHA256 `aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a`、shared control SHA256 `3d289c2bef1c562b26f9b713c2a826b813a286a1f7e21f9ead0a01c63c5e0d80`、Direct-HDR reference manifest SHA256 `86cf6c0801365c0c1e5949e4e04d3178fa03f334997db6d09547f69a2b5af37c`；Ours/V2E checkpoint SHA256 `6ecea6a4520ae09b8cc99ebfd8da7a6a324363d2e57dbf73790e27ee2dbec8d2 / a7140b63f3b5042b0dc0ed7cc3aa2974c4e9859db919f318826358883f7ce8a0`。
- g1 工作树继续复用既有 `genesis-event-camera-plugin-r1-cadence-sweep-20261019d`，从 `2690935` 前进到 `2d782fb`；没有创建第二个 worktree。运行时 GPU0 为 idle renderer 选用，其余已占用 GPU 和其他用户进程未触碰。Genesis 输出共享 Quadrants cache-lock warning，运行仍 exit 0；未清理共享缓存。
- 复跑最新端点隔离：

  ```bash
  cd /home/zhaoyuhan/Eventbased_WAM/repositories/genesis-event-camera-plugin-r1-cadence-sweep-20261019d
  bash benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/runtime_logs/r1_5_endpoint_terminal_cadence_isolation_20261019e.launch.sh
  ```

**建议与未解决项：**

目前不把块定位归结为一个已完全定位的 bug，也不建议切换 frozen core 或立即重训。可提交的最小精确端点 NumPy/Torch patch 是一般性正确性修复候选，`git apply --check` 通过，但先交研究侧审查，并界定所有旧 Ours 插值事件及其模型结果必须作为旧 core 版本单独标识。主要事件分布差异仍来自端点之间的生成路径；下一步只在 benchmark 诊断层增加“区间内亮度连续变化 + 遮挡/对应不确定像素请求 direct render”的候选，对两 episodes 的静态、平滑亮度 ramp、真实中间运动和 21 query phases 比较图像、逐子帧/总事件量与成本。若这组一般性控制通过且事件量明显接近 direct reference，再考虑经审查的 core 改动与一次匹配小重训；否则先把最小复现和替代路径交研究侧决定。闭环成功、真实事件硬件保真、正式 Ours/V2E 排名目前都没有由本轮实验建立。

#### R1.5：连续表面采样候选验证结果（2026-10-21）

本阶段针对研究侧复现的“连续斜面微小投影移动却出现大亮度跳变”实现并验证了 benchmark 独立 surface-raster 候选。候选将同标签且局部三维连续的相邻深度样本连接成投影三角形，以透视校正插值和深度 z-buffer 覆盖像素；不会把近邻样本放大成整像素遮挡。事件生成配置、阈值及 radiance 输入保持不变。`genesis_event_plugin/` frozen core 未修改。

**通用数值控制（g1 实测，通过）：** 六项检查均通过：静止零事件；增/减深度斜面在 `±1e-5 px` 微动时内部 1984/1984 像素均没有 `>0.2` log 跳变（最大误差 `1.55e-6`）；解析 `0.25X+Z=1` 斜面相机双向横移 `1 mm/40 ms`，真实亮度变化 `0.03025/0.03053 < 0.2` 且事件数均为 0；平滑 `±0.5` log ramp 在 4/8 ms 产生阈值事件，最大时序误差 `1.26 ns`；前景/背景重叠由 z-buffer 正确选择较近的前景；双端点都无表面支持的像素保持 NaN 并请求恰好一次直渲。六项输出 [`controls.json`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_surface_raster_controls_20261021b/controls.json) SHA256 `def75a55391683b89411ed976709faa0427fe68711666a6e63621cae2c4c9f23`；输出清单 SHA256 `d51868212410f4c289624a6141f1c5943698314baa8198ee1f53744f29c1a545`。g1 Numba 总耗时 `1.349 s`。此前五项首次 JIT 运行 `20261021a` 仍保留，其首轮总耗时 `5.035 s`，不作为稳态性能数据。

单像素 unknown 控制最初先在本机 NumPy fallback 通过，结果仍保留在 [`local unknown smoke`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_surface_raster_unknown_request_local_smoke_20261021a/README.md)；随后已使用同一控制脚本在 g1 的 Numba 后端复跑并纳入上面的六项结果。新增控制代码来自 commit `8c84e3b`，当前脚本 SHA256 `4ad85279ba11046edaf862174820bd59e0de911bdb54d93350655881ef513d16`；g1 隔离 worktree 只替换并校验了该脚本，候选实现和配置未改。

上述附加控制与结果由 commit `8c84e3b` 交付；当前控制脚本 SHA256 `4ad85279ba11046edaf862174820bd59e0de911bdb54d93350655881ef513d16`。g1 五项控制的旧脚本 SHA256 仍按其实际执行版本保留在上方，二者不可混为一次运行。

**真实缓存短片段（g1 实测，候选未通过非退化验收）：** 重放中心 episode 004 与 `+55 mm` episode 005 的全部已记录 trace 行；每集 47 个状态，真实时间间隔为 0.5–1 ms、有效均值约 1.15 kHz，没有合成不存在的物理状态。每集 42 个非端点中间时刻都触发整帧 direct-render fallback。候选每帧平均请求像素分别为 16.493% 和 16.113%；其中无任一表面覆盖的区域为 14.907% 和 14.549%，单侧覆盖为 1.586% 和 1.565%，共面标签冲突为 0。由于 renderer 只有整帧接口，任一请求都使该帧全量直渲。

启用 fallback 后，候选和 exact logged-state Direct-HDR 的输出 raw event 数组逐元素完全相同：episode 004 为 2,807 个、episode 005 为 4,618 个。这说明最终输出没有原 warp 伪影，但原因是两个短片段的所有中间帧都直接渲染；不能据此声称 surface-raster 已消除真实片段异常。fallback 前，在已覆盖像素上图像误差较小（逐帧有效区域 log-error P95 均值 `6.04e-5 / 4.37e-4`），但约 15% 画面仍无可用表面支持，必须保持 unknown/fallback。

| 每集 47 状态 | 004 中心 | 005 +55 mm |
|---|---:|---:|
| 候选 full-frame fallback | 42/42 | 42/42 |
| 候选总生产估时 | 4.925 s | 4.230 s |
| exact direct render + DVS | 1.369 s | 1.552 s |
| 相对 direct 路径 | 3.60× | 2.72× |

在这两条片段上该候选不仅退化成全量中间帧直渲，而且比 direct 路径更慢，现状不适合作为生产重建器。旧 Ours checkpoint 在上述相同 direct-event 输入的 14 个唯一 query 上平均 XY 误差为 64.2 mm（最大 167.1 mm）；旧 V2E checkpoint 为 8.9 mm（最大 14.4 mm）。这些是旧权重诊断，不是新训练成绩。鉴于候选输出逐元素等同 reference、且计算更贵，本阶段没有执行新训练或闭环，避免把 direct-render 训练结果误报为 raster 修复带来的稳定感知。

**版本和数据：** 候选代码 commit `59bedc52e70c80611748cf15d00111128efabe46`；scorer 边界修复 `a5388db102da884bf3e05da0f19f55d1d67dbcc3`；g1 supervised launcher commit `a92f25019982affb34427631e1c6921bf9ad4aa6`（run 中实际执行 commit）；frozen core base `e79e656ac00f7847b98db9e12bf43b093f3afff1`，无 core diff。配置 `r1_5_surface_raster_candidate_20261021a.json` SHA256 `eb7278f69c6a7dc586e3254356b22ee121cb90219244a3ad45862586811adc05`。实现 SHA256 `74b18a926f33a70740bbdc44a33d543b9c3ee9d7e99df4b2649e0bd3f04b8dd5`，replay 脚本 SHA256 `1eca8a4ed75f00efb5f4cc6d7d208223f9b206ccf7400e2201fe18d912571335`，控制脚本 SHA256 `875eea94acbb07596a1176f612b86720a0be5c1b8205b094e4c0e4e7fd69290b`。episode 004/005 的 trace SHA256 为 `3f07e4e7a0846827be7a3d5decf7c6197d77ca0237bacff55f2c45a70a04349c` / `f0f556842029cd54b3fc082ee0e2848dd194f3322a8a9c0769c2c889a6f6ae5d`；source-cache SHA256 为 `0525c18c8d89f5831ef0cb71ac9e3b6b130d60579daf374f06d5cfcee5b879d7` / `91c1397642e317d6bb51150b4107c518b52e4fa9fcf5f1caf6a2a91315c7d434`；render-evidence SHA256 为 `b3afe5b5badee3d2606d343bb750aa970887928c41b8001a5fc4e9ff32b9c321` / `f161079a24c1bf9fee838a6fbb2a0fa5d785435b116f0ea5628e6efbba497236`。输出清单 SHA256 `07a1009c6b7ab382f35a50fde57f5f7a24a4e0a90c8e899924f1ccf16f3c9730`，summary SHA256 `6ab3cece2029ae4ae852dd753eab1475d25a8ce6f2299d5f0d2aedc9610dbc76`。

复跑指引、逐帧误差、分区事件数、query 记录和输出限制见 [`R1.5 cached replay report`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_surface_raster_cached_replay_20261021b/README.md)。`20261021a/` 保留因浮点末端 timestamp 边界提前中止的部分文件，并有 README 标明不可用于结论。

**当前结论与下一步：** 采样反例在独立连续面控制中已修复；真实短片段的最终事件异常只通过每个中间时刻整帧直渲被消除，因此候选尚未证明能以重建方式解决实片段。没有新数据训练、未选候选 episode 定位稳定性验证，也没有短闭环恢复。建议保持 frozen core 与旧结果不变，下一轮只针对这两集约 15% 的无表面支持区改善表面覆盖/未知区判定，并在目标片段达到低 fallback 率且成本优于 direct-render 后，才做原预算新训练和未选 episode 验证；目前不推荐切换 frozen core、不推荐开始 Ours/V2E 排名。若找不到能保证遮挡正确且降低未知区的候选，应把高频 Direct-HDR 限定为 reference，并另行讨论研究上可接受的生成路径。

#### R1.5 multi-reference surface completion candidate (2026-10-21; g1 clip result pending)

The next candidate targets the measured `14.5–14.9%` no-source pixels using a geometry-based method common in depth-image view synthesis: use already rendered complementary reference views to recover visible surfaces, then decide visibility by depth. Li, Zhu and Sun describe selective use of neighboring complementary views to reduce disocclusion holes while avoiding a full warp of every additional view; temporal view-synthesis work also warns that unsupported hole filling can create geometric or temporal artifacts. We therefore do not spatially inpaint: every output surface must trace to an observed radiance/depth/segmentation sample, and equal-depth label conflicts or truly unobserved pixels still request a direct render. Sources: [Li et al., 2018](https://arxiv.org/abs/1802.03079); [Kanchana et al., WACV 2022](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/WACV2022/papers/Kanchana_Revealing_Disocclusions_in_Temporal_View_Synthesis_Through_Infilling_Vector_Prediction_WACV_2022_paper.pdf).

**Implementation and preliminary controls:** commit `1605c6e54aea72473e222d0f01d2bd6a26f00e82`, pushed to `exp/robot-closed-loop-v1`. Frozen simulator core is untouched. Static fixed-camera pixels are reused from any already rendered 100 Hz source view; only target/Panda link labels are projected from their source body transforms to the interpolated target body transforms and rasterized. A per-pixel nearest-depth test composes dynamic and static surfaces. Radiance is log-interpolated only between observations of the same frontmost label/depth; one-sided observations and unknown regions are counted, with unknown/ambiguous geometry requesting full-frame render. Rasterization scans each moving label's source bounding box to reduce work.

Seven small CPU controls pass locally: moving-only projection exactly matches the existing general SE(3) projector on 24 samples; a static surface revealed in one complementary view is recovered; moving-over-static and static-over-moving choose the nearer surface; equal-depth different-label conflicts request render; same-surface log-radiance ramps interpolate correctly; and never-observed pixels remain NaN/render requests. Runtime `0.0041 s`; NumPy fallback only. The structured output is archived in [`local controls`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_multiref_surface_controls_local_20261021c/README.md), with controls SHA256 `c967926022c884027108e70131a6c9f4e3d6c4836cc75f1ecf309d732925beb2`. This is not the g1 cached clip validation.

Files/configuration: [`r1_5_multiref_surface_candidate.py`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/diagnostics/r1_5_multiref_surface_candidate.py), [`run_r1_5_multiref_surface_cached_replay.py`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/diagnostics/run_r1_5_multiref_surface_cached_replay.py), [`run_r1_5_multiref_surface_controls.py`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/diagnostics/run_r1_5_multiref_surface_controls.py), config [`r1_5_multiref_surface_candidate_20261021c.json`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/configs/r1_5_multiref_surface_candidate_20261021c.json). SHA256s, respectively: `2bfe5046db81769bb26562f727b3dc44d95e9eb4884c73ab355c7d4e335b9772`, `0ee24a75bd21429c75c6a8d32a7ede7cc4220f69073db2cb4637ae096e04ffc7`, `a6656c5887056cc54589bd8c309fd04aec4187e52cddae2f2ae1aef73164c773`, and `db23df18bc103cffc0f8bddec883c0a40eba6cc56506c9d980c04b09d45e1b55`. The shared cropped raster helper hash is `46d9c99cfe9d8efbf64d554f3585c488f689cf742357d3fa05d0d4241c6ae0db`.

**Next candidate (`20261021e`, underway):** the d replay repeated source-side camera-ray transforms and triangle connectivity/bounding-box setup for every query. Seven controls pass at code commit `4a4e51f717b9f177223ab99598e42571fc632f64`; output is archived in [`20261021e local controls`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_multiref_surface_controls_local_20261021e/README.md), controls SHA256 `7d3d7d4f15ce2fbfadc8d1c4f1e2762071f13d9eace8e89cfc79229ff297faf3`. The next g1 replay uses a fresh output directory, saves per-frame unknown masks, shows the pre-fallback reconstruction in the montage, and splits CPU time across projection, surface rasterization and multi-reference composition. This is an implementation optimization only; it does not relax support, occlusion, brightness, scene, controller, or scorer rules. It has not yet run on g1.

**Not yet established:** whether this complementary-view candidate reduces real-clip unsupported coverage, preserves event timing/occlusion, or beats the direct-render cost. No g1 data, model inference, retraining, or closed-loop result exists for commit `1605c6e` yet. Do not treat the local synthetic controls as evidence of clip-level success. If the candidate still leaves per-frame full-render requests or is slower than exact direct rendering, profile the moving-label path and constrain support further before considering any frozen-core proposal.

#### R1.5 surface coverage/cost closeout (2026-10-21)

This section supersedes the preceding “Next candidate (`20261021e`, underway)” note. The subsequent scissor and thread profiles are complete; none meets both event-fidelity and runtime goals.

- **Geometry and requests:** Across 47 actual query rows per episode, the surface candidate requested scissored exact HDR repair for 0.913% of pixels in episode 004 and 0.951% in 005 (per-row maximum 1.048% / 1.095%); no full-frame fallback occurred. Crop rectangles covered 24.7% / 25.8% of full-frame pixels over 42 reconstructed rows. Minimum supported segmentation agreement was 99.9987% / 99.9895%; same-label depth P99 max was 0.0048 / 0.0190 mm. Across 42 frame metrics, dynamic occlusion misses were 34/21, false occlusions zero, and wrong-link pixels 0/101. These errors are retained rather than described as perfect occlusion.
- **Events and localization:** Exact event matches in the repaired/requested region were 608/608 and 905/907. In the supported warped region they were only 4/2,199 and 0/3,711. Candidate/direct event totals were 1,774/2,807 and 3,106/4,618; five-bin voxel cosine averaged 0.873 / 0.823. With the unchanged old Ours checkpoint, only seven query estimates per clip were available: candidate-event XY median was 12.7 mm at center and 243.6 mm at +55 mm (maximum 405.6 mm). This is diagnostic-only and does not qualify as a new training or test result.
- **Cost:** Nine paired profiles U–AE all remained slower than Direct-HDR. The lowest observed batch ratio was 1.36× for 16-thread CPU profile AA (4.293 s vs 3.151 s; the direct side of this run was unusually slow). The latest CPU equivalence profile AC was 2.81× (6.107 s vs 2.172 s). PyTorch CUDA triangle scatter AE was 5.17× (13.540 s vs 2.619 s); warm episode 005 alone remained 1.68×. Y’s optional NumPy query-index audit time was removed by a separate correction addendum; its original result and manifest remain unchanged.
- **CUDA check:** Support and label rasters matched the CPU serial result in all 168 calls; floating outputs differed by at most `1.19e-7` luminance and `5.96e-8 m` depth. The candidate was not bit-identical and did not improve event matches. Genesis HDR overlay code was loaded from `.venv-robot` with CUDA Torch 2.5.1+cu121; Genesis warned that this Torch version is unsupported. All five endpoint plus ten dense-frame saved-cache rerender checks were exactly zero-error in each episode. A stock conda Genesis attempt failed before rendering due to the missing HDR overlay method; a Numba CUDA probe failed because the installed CUDA 10.1/NVVM could not compile a device kernel. The successful renderer/evidence run did not modify any shared environment or frozen core.
- **Artifacts:** Full AA/AC/AE and corrected Y outputs, all verified against their result manifests, are retained in the branch under `benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/`. Compact profiles and copied g1 manifests are in [`R1.5 closeout`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_surface_cost_closeout_20261021/README.md). AE ran code commit `1f5c1815490f45d00ef61f6d6f167ae31f614017`, config SHA256 `1ce44bca85f2970acaa91ea4298e9e991bf3c55fbd420025894bbe11a6fcf702`, output-manifest SHA256 `75aa1a4c0374b1fd1c9ee9a9d5f954cbbc4dd112057780c332b42c43d4e64cb8`; run.log SHA256 `e0768a03a59164fb7c707239955d3a9d9e12a68774feb5030e0219bd7bc71765`, exit 0, PID 2337284. Its full server result is at `~/Eventbased_WAM/repositories/genesis-event-camera-plugin-r1_5_multiref_scissor_20261021ae/benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_multiref_scissor_candidate_20261021ae/`. Reproduction command is in the closeout README.
- **Not done:** No candidate passed both event fidelity and cost; no retraining, new-episode validation, or short robot closed loop was run. Keep the candidate out of the frozen core. Research-session choice requested: stop warp optimization and use exact Direct-HDR as the R1 reference, or approve one bounded benchmark-layer experiment using the renderer’s GPU triangle path. The second choice changes the implementation direction; it should be bounded by an early event-fidelity/cost gate.

The DVS sees every pixel; its silhouette band is used only after event generation. The two cached robot episodes remain unchanged.
- **Audit output:** every radiance draw records actual GL vendor/renderer/version, `GL_MULTISAMPLE` state, draw FBO, sample-buffer/sample counts and sample positions. The experiment now expects 84 audited HDR passes total: 56 across cached episodes 004/005 and 28 for the simple control. Existing output `20261025a` was never created or overwritten.
- **Rendering contract note:** the Khronos OpenGL 3.0 specification says disabling `MULTISAMPLE` makes rasterization equivalent to fragment-center single-sample coverage while retaining full fragment coverage. Thus this factor changes rasterization behavior while leaving Genesis' multisample FBO allocation intact; it is not a comparison against a separately allocated single-sample FBO ([Khronos OpenGL 3.0 specification](https://registry.khronos.org/OpenGL/specs/gl/glspec30.pdf)).
- **Checks and limits:** Python AST parsing, JSON parsing, and `git diff --check` passed. No Genesis renderer, GPU query, or experiment was run in this turn. #### R1.5 CPU-only renderer feasibility probe (2026-10-28)

- **Request and scope:** at the user's request, tried a bounded one-frame software-render smoke on g1, using the staged candidate worktree at code commit `e81852e`. Genesis initialized with `gs.cpu` on one-thread CPU settings. The probe used a 64x64 plane+box scene and forced Mesa/llvmpipe (`CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=`, `EGL_PLATFORM=surfaceless`, `PYOPENGL_PLATFORM=egl`, `LIBGL_ALWAYS_SOFTWARE=1`, `GALLIUM_DRIVER=llvmpipe`, `LP_NUM_THREADS=1`, and the installed `50_mesa.json` EGL vendor manifest).
- **Result:** Genesis reached scene visualizer initialization but failed before the first image with `EGLError: No EGL context could be initialized`; exit code 1, about 40 s. Thus `gs.cpu` selects CPU physics but does not provide the required raster renderer. The pinned Genesis pyrender package contains an OSMesa platform, but g1 has no OSMesa shared library and no `Xvfb`/`xvfb-run`; no supported CPU software context was available. No frame or event result was produced, and this is not a substitute for the NVIDIA renderer comparison.
- **Evidence:** g1 log `/home/zhaoyuhan/Eventbased_WAM/repositories/gec-robot-r1_5-20261026-e81852e/benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_software_gl_cpu_probe_20261028_g1/probe.log`, SHA256 `6ef93c197244055ce0922825c764912dab3e62eb2097d9a60c57dae7152d237d`. Genesis also reported failure to acquire the shared Taichi kernel-cache lock; no cache-clean or cache modification command was run. The unique probe directory is separate from the still-absent factorial output.
- **g3 fallback check:** verified the host key from `/mnt/c/Users/zhaoyuhan/.ssh/known_hosts` and used strict checking. The first read-only connection did not return; after waiting over 60 s, one verification attempt was closed before key exchange by the remote host. No g3 resource command ran; stop further SSH attempts under the current cooldown rule. g3 software-renderer availability remains unknown.
- **Conclusion/next step:** CPU-only execution is not currently viable on g1 for this renderer. Keep the scientific factorial gated on a safely assigned GPU; revisit g3 only after the SSH cooldown and with its software-renderer libraries checked, or use an explicitly provisioned software-GL environment. Do not install system libraries or alter the shared Python environment for this probe.

Its `/home` project path is NFS-mounted from `172.16.1.21:/export/home`; no Slurm commands were present. g3 GPU0 (RTX 4090, UUID `GPU-1c25cab2-5cc0-4f29-fb76-15da94f694d9`) had no compute process, 39 MiB allocated, and 0% utilization at the final check, so the user-reported allocation was applied to that device.
- **g3 outcome:** the detached process exited `139` at `2026-10-28T16:40:58Z`; no diagnostic frames/results were written. Its log SHA256 is `c60e45f743c9433b0a27816ed18fa2d1574a699192ca1b6b8163ed3e15c05351`. A two-cycle faulthandler smoke exited `139` while Genesis was building the first robot scene; its script/log SHA256s are `dfcf69d5c2e498d51c58f740f79e777405b2b7684e7598c2e4a9b8b1a80f6ff8` / `b9a49b989aab8ce1a4c0ae02b2c133905f39762cb7a3a4ee58cc4b46de40b76c`. A separate no-scene EGL probe also segfaulted inside `eglGetProcAddress` (exit `139`), so g3's EGL path is currently unusable for this renderer. The robot venv includes system site packages; PyOpenGL resolves from the base Conda environment while Genesis resolves from `.venv-robot`. This path mix is a possible contributor, not yet the proven root cause. No shared environment, driver, cache, or system library was changed.
- **g1 outcome:** once GPU0 became idle again, the original runner rendered the first condition but exited `1` on its own resolve-count assertion before writing result files. Log SHA256 `b3b7eb0eae35aa278daf1a9fea1cf7d7570a5e35298d239c138c642ae78747a8`; run-metadata SHA256 `59b1438354b5787196e77a4e0e8d3a9c5a18a4f3f792c38f7b6d6d6b90a7554a`. Inspection of the installed Genesis 1.2.2 overlay showed why: `Camera.render(rgb=True, depth=True, segmentation=True, radiance=True)` performs the radiance+depth resolve as separate color and depth blits, then runs segmentation as a distinct pass. The old check incorrectly required one blit. It also sampled the draw-FBO state after readback had rebound the single-sample framebuffer.

Runner SHA256 is `b1065522331f7d9ebe0b21b30159dd998bab0b84eaedbf44d54ef7b32df19aa7`. Static syntax and `git diff --check` passed. This corrects the diagnostic audit only; frozen core and render behavior are unchanged.

Runner SHA256 `312b23cae19af9cf35c758da51ecd0da3a24285bf80a2755130d8d9debb7c646`; experiment config SHA256 `ced55b5d57953107b7c0ba91d36b06e5a8e23736b15d666ab177dae3ebcb32af`; scene config SHA256 `aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a`.

Physics was not advanced. GL audit confirms `GL_MULTISAMPLE` was actually toggled while the same four-sample Genesis HDR FBO allocation remained in place. On the two robot clips, shadow-on produced 200/236 fixed-floor events; shadow-off produced zero. Shadow changed more than 0.2 log luminance on about 9.5%/11.2% of fixed-floor pixels. Toggling multisampling had effectively zero fixed-floor log-luminance effect, while full-frame event counts fell from 97→40 and 513→413 on episode 004, and 613→252 and 927→615 on episode 005 (MS off→on, shadow off/on respectively). The simple-contour control also completed. This supports a real shadow-driven floor-lighting contribution and an MSAA-dependent edge-event contribution in these development clips; it does not establish that either alone explains the old model's localization failures.

The six cached input hashes are unchanged from the preceding R1.5 record. The local result-manifest check passed for every copied file.

#### R1.5 matched shadow causal replay on robot clips (2026-10-29)

Thus shadow-off is strongly out of domain for this old checkpoint in these clips. This does not establish that shadows are the sole cause of earlier +55 mm error spikes or that shadows generally improve perception.

#### R1.5 exact-seed source reproduction (2026-10-29; completed on g1)

- **Result:** the original episode-005 R0.5 run was rerun with seed `2026103425`, +55 mm launch offset, and the original 2 kHz callback. All 1,459 saved state rows match exactly: target position/velocity, Panda qpos/qvel, TCP, contacts, and the full state-trace SHA are identical. More strongly, the complete 11,937,381-byte raw event NPZ has the same SHA as the archived event file (`b698817a…a7b2b6`); the full trace SHA is likewise identical (`310837ea…bff28c`). All 6,182 events in 0.5505–0.5905 s match one-to-one by pixel/polarity/time within 0.5 ms, with zero time error. The installed Genesis renderer-overlay hashes also match the archived run manifest. This rules out changed seed, physical trajectory, corrupted archive, and a renderer-overlay update as explanations for the archived failure.
- **Same-checkpoint replay:** after preserving event timestamps as float64 and using the exact final E cutoff (`0.5904999999999999`), the seven query voxel hashes match E's saved archived comparison exactly. Old Ours mean XY error is `58.692 mm` on archive (p95 `108.332 mm`) and `10.641 mm` on E shadow-on events (p95 `17.185 mm`); state availability is `7/7` for both. Auxiliary velocity error differs in the other direction: `16.546` vs `37.365 mm/s`. This confirms that the strong sensitivity is spatial/source-specific rather than a uniform change to all predicted state fields. The first compact extraction lost precision at 5 ms edges and is not used; the retained v3 evidence keeps float64 and exact query timestamps.

#### R1.5 cached cadence/MSAA localization diagnosis (2026-10-29; completed)

- **Completed:** `r1_5_cached_state_cadence_renderer_20261029c` replayed the same captured 81 true states, without stepping physics. It compared the selected 47-frame cadence/MSAA-on stream, the dense 81-frame 2 kHz/MSAA-on stream, and dense 2 kHz with `GL_MULTISAMPLE` disabled. The same old Ours checkpoint was evaluated at the same seven causal cutoffs for all streams.
- **Localization:** archive event stream mean XY error was `58.69 mm` (p95 `108.33 mm`); the three cached streams were `10.64/17.19`, `12.08/21.29`, and `15.71/21.13 mm` mean/p95 respectively. All were valid at 7/7 cutoffs. Thus the old checkpoint's large error is strongly sensitive to the event source path. Denser cached sampling improves archive event match from 56.0% to 74.8% and reduces velocity mean error from `37.37` to `22.15 mm/s`, but does not close the position gap. Turning off GL multisampling raises the cached event count from `5,899` to `7,123` and lowers archive match to 54.0%; it does not explain the full failure. This is one +55 mm clip and one old checkpoint, not a general capability estimate.
- **Renderer/cost:** per cached condition, scene build took `47.90–52.74 s`, render `0.97–1.55 s`, and DVS `0.67–1.66 s`; all three conditions took `168.31 s` wall including repeated builds. Genesis retained its 4-sample FBO in both settings; the off condition only disabled GL multisample rasterization. Segmentation was identical; `0.77%` of pixels exceeded `0.2` absolute log-luma difference. No frozen-core change.
- **Evidence/provenance:** report and raw downloaded frames/events are in [`r1_5_cached_state_cadence_renderer_20261029c`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_cached_state_cadence_renderer_20261029c/README.md), with `SHA256SUMS`. #### R1.5 online HDR/cache replay consistency and checkpoint localization (2026-10-29; completed)

- **Online capture completed:** `r1_5_online_hdr_capture_20261029d` finished successfully on g1. It reran runner commit `2100645b0473c294760789bc4475b644f261d9cf`, seed `2026103425`, 0.4 m/s, +55 mm, and reproduced the archived state trace/raw events exactly. A read-only hook copied the already-rendered `DirectHdrEventSource.last_hdr`; the hook added zero physics steps, controls, or camera renders. The 81 float32 HDR frames cover `[0.5505,0.5905] s` at 0.5 ms; HDR SHA256 `986cbf19c6297b4d55db2d1e5ad7634f23c0c19634faa4c4e02bf2689a0e8c1c`. The original capture used GPU0 RTX 3090 UUID `GPU-fd9f1bd4-83c4-cd74-0c88-241db6ec7e23`, ran about 4m51s, and released its reservation. Source trace/events SHA256 `310837ea07ab2f819f590ccce91b3952dd9e85ea5005537bc1f4d1a339bff28c` / `b698817a8347ca48ad75015f0cb81c3bfc711958a60d3387d468c70456a7b2b6`.
- **First confirmed generator/replay defect:** at all 81 shared timestamps, online and cached MSAA-on HDR arrays are bitwise identical. Nevertheless, cold cached replay has 5899 events while the online event source has 6179 events in the window excluding its start. The cold replay resets the DVS comparator at `0.5505 s`, omitting its prior causal state. Reconstructing the comparator in the exact 0.5 ms sample order from the launch HDR and 324885 preceding events, then using the same online `_hdr_to_log_gray` transform, reproduces all `6179/6179` window events with zero timestamp difference. Cold physical-log replay reproduces its saved cached stream exactly (`5899/5899`). Therefore the main event-count mismatch is lost comparator history, not different HDR pixels.
- **Transform sensitivity separated:** with the same historical event counts, two physical-log baseline mappings produced 6276 and 6431 events; each matched 6175/6179 online events at the same pixel/polarity within 0.5 ms (99.94% source coverage). Extra unmatched candidates were 101–256. Matched timestamp error was sub-microsecond for almost all events; the largest matched offset was `0.00654 ms` for launch-baseline mapping and `0.368 ms` for exact-residual mapping. Online-vs-physical per-frame log difference mean/P95/max is `8.12e-6/2.76e-5/7.80e-5`. This is a small but nonzero transform effect, sensitive to comparator initialization near threshold; it is much smaller than cold-reset history mismatch and is not a fully independent physical-log history replay. Early scratch variants that mixed the online comparator baseline with physical-log values caused a spurious first-frame pulse and are excluded.
- **Model still fails on the correct source:** the same old checkpoint on the exact history-seeded online events remains at `58.69 mm` mean XY error / `108.33 mm` P95, velocity error `16.55 mm/s` mean, and state valid at `7/7` adjacent cutoffs. Its predicted x jumps from `564 mm` to `475 mm` to `520 mm` while GT remains near `576 mm`. This points to unstable spatial localization / source-domain mismatch after event generation; the replay defect does not fully explain or fix model localization. The seven cutoffs are one clip, not seven independent episodes.
- **Evidence and provenance:** report, replay script, CSV, all three raw event arrays and SHA256 manifest are in [`r1_5_online_hdr_capture_20261029d`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_online_hdr_capture_20261029d/README.md). HDR capture driver commit `bac8463fea68dd0ae78b193190f2407e9f3ed85f`, launcher commit `197e68ec69419fde0b421a2fa55acfcf3c5e5c7a`; final diagnostic script commit `dd42abb`; file SHA256 `b6acac82d6878645eaa70917c3674e2cb0bd416e1a1c173f7106165f65f1958c`; state-mapped summary SHA256 `39c56ddd9e2e271cd4bb4a9cc0ffce3a0cc8b190b06ba54bd07456f7aa2ef969`; state-mapped events SHA256 `4b6a899304d6f73891a1a1cd7d78545d5255e392b8c99491226a4bf51057849f`; earlier base-state comparison commit `9b3c2f3`; config SHA256 `0609f01207ceb7679771a53296607bc3a038ba34af5f703079834dde5638c6cd`; scene SHA256 `aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a`; DVS implementation SHA256 `914dc83525ff96dc2c1a1373819de74491cfbb03007b65e8c93609f6cd1cf638`; frozen core `e79e656ac00f7847b98db9e12bf43b093f3afff1`. Full HDR remains accessible at `/home/zhaoyuhan/Eventbased_WAM/repositories/gec-robot-r0_5-39b235a/benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_online_hdr_capture_20261029d/online_hdr_capture/episode_000/online_hdr_frames_2khz.npz` (SHA256 above); it was not copied into Git.
- **Training-path audit complete:** both the display-PNG checkpoint and native-HDR checkpoint generate DVS events continuously over each full episode after one `start_episode()`; neither training pipeline resets comparator state per query/window. The cold reset was specific to the short cached replay diagnostic. Old checkpoint `d391fec…e804e9` used display PNG grayscale; native checkpoint `6ecea6a…dbec8d2` used scene-linear radiance with `physical_log`.
- **Next:** inspect the heatmap-to-position decoder before retraining. On this one +55 mm Direct-HDR clip, global soft-argmax is much less stable than selecting the heatmap's largest mode. Verify the offline decoding alternatives on existing shared development inputs first; do not change controller behavior or training budget from a single clip.

#### R1.5 heatmap ambiguity and decoder diagnostic (2026-10-29; completed, offline)

- **Finding:** the same 6,179 history-seeded Direct-HDR events and seven causal windows were evaluated with the old display-PNG checkpoint and the native-HDR checkpoint. In the worst native-HDR window (`0.5875 s`), the current global soft-argmax had `137.5 mm` XY error, while projecting only the highest heatmap cell to the fixed planar target height gave `18.5 mm`; soft-argmax restricted to a 5×5-cell patch around that peak gave `14.8 mm`. Across seven adjacent cutoffs, native full-map soft-argmax mean/max was `43.5/137.5 mm`, highest-mode projection `18.1/19.1 mm`, and local soft-argmax `14.4/18.6 mm`. Old PNG checkpoint changed from `58.7/111.1 mm` to `10.0/19.1 mm` and `9.7/12.8 mm`. These are decoder-only offline counterfactuals, not a validated controller change.
- **Input support:** a post-inference projected 4 cm cube ROI expanded by 6 pixels contained `491–534` events per 25 ms window (`13.1–13.9%` of events). Native heatmap probability inside that ROI fell to `54.7%` at the worst cutoff; the top mode stayed within about `5.2 px` of the GT projection. This points to a multimodal/diffuse heatmap being averaged into a bad location; it does not prove the event source is sufficient or identify which scene features create the secondary modes.
- **Domain check:** the native checkpoint's separate validation episode 005 is seed `2026104135`, `0.4 m/s`, center; its own native-HDR event windows at `0.55–0.60 s` have errors `7.0, 22.0, 26.2, 24.8, 2.6, 1.6 mm`. The direct test is seed `2026103425`, same speed but `+55 mm`. These episodes are not physically matched, so the contrast is descriptive only.
- **Artifacts/provenance:** CPU-only WSL run, `0.16 s`, no GPU, renderer, physics or training. Diagnostic script `benchmark/robot_closed_loop/diagnostics/inspect_r1_5_heatmap_evidence.py`, SHA256 `105e0a3f404bc3650766a8741fd8392690133aaabcec12f92ae3f76188c3d378`; run HEAD `67c92a1` with this working-tree script hash. Query CSV SHA256 `292d9ebd0ad1256d051e46bc0aa789b2c369e6ba45017f7e8f5b93f241c9ace3`; direct event SHA256 `93d17785b5c408aad543c542f9e1f37367c055f4d7ac9aa1a805e54c086761eb`; scene SHA256 `aa97f6f5a12af92561ef053407bb795bba2801582958b4ad1134bcd24497435a`; old/native checkpoint SHA256 `d391fec293e2c6c50cdb267e15f742ed09e77e7b28e56a79fdf700f633e804e9` / `6ecea6a4520ae09b8cc99ebfd8da7a6a324363d2e57dbf73790e27ee2dbec8d2`. CSV/summary and their hashes are in [`heatmap_spatial_signature_20261029b`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_online_hdr_capture_20261029d/heatmap_spatial_signature_20261029b/).
- **Next:** apply the same decoder-only comparison to the existing full shared Direct-HDR development episodes and native-HDR held-out episodes, with per-episode/position-bin summaries. If peak-based decoding consistently removes jumps without increasing false localization, run a small replay through the existing predictor offline before considering any closed-loop change. Frozen core, task, controller, and training budget remain unchanged.

#### Native HDR validation decoder check (2026-10-29; completed, pre-contact only)

- **Coverage:** original native-HDR validation split, episodes 004/005/006: seed/speed/y = `2026104134 / 0.30 m/s / +55 mm`, `2026104135 / 0.40 m/s / 0 mm`, and `2026104136 / 0.50 m/s / −55 mm`. The phase filter retained 91/58/45 pre-contact queries; all contact-to-lift and post-lift rows were excluded because fixed-plane XY decoding is not valid once the target leaves z=20 mm.
- **Per-episode XY error mean/P95:** global soft-argmax `4.93/12.53`, `2.10/4.61`, `3.82/7.40 mm`; heatmap argmax `6.22/14.05`, `3.93/7.22`, `4.69/9.51 mm`; local soft-argmax `4.89/12.61`, `2.11/4.73`, `3.83/7.44 mm`. Across 194 correlated windows the three means/P95 are `3.83/10.14`, `5.18/11.07`, `3.81/10.30 mm`.
- **Interpretation:** native-domain validation shows argmax is not a universal fix; the existing global decoder is already accurate there. Local soft-argmax is nearly unchanged on these native inputs while reducing the single Direct-HDR +55 mm spike, so continue with offline paired evaluation on the complete shared Direct-HDR development set before considering any decoder change.
- **Artifacts/provenance:** per-episode CSV/summary/hash manifests are under [`native_validation_episode004_006_decoder_20261029d`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_online_hdr_capture_20261029d/native_validation_episode004_006_decoder_20261029d/) and [`native_validation_episode005_decoder_20261029c`](../../benchmark/robot_closed_loop/results/r1_5_online_hdr_capture_20261029d/native_validation_episode005_decoder_20261029c/). g1 CPU only; inference times 3.05/1.49/1.77 s.
